### Task 12: Deploy, rebuild, verify live, tick scenario, branch summary (operational)

No code. Every step runs from the repo root, because the `fantasy` profile is keyed by working
directory. Never print, paste or commit the sheet id: read it from `.env` into a shell variable.
Everything these steps write goes under `data/draft-board/` (gitignored).

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/project-updates/2026-09-12-local-draft-board-branch-summary.md`
- Modify: `docs/bugs/2026-09-12-reorder-refresh-tick-misattachment.md` (C1 closed in the sheet, Step 11)
- Writes, never committed: `scripts/draft-board/Data.gs`, `data/draft-board/board - <date>.json`,
  `data/draft-board/pulls/*`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every task above; `playwright-cli -s=fantasy`; `.env` `DRAFT_SHEET_ID`.
- Produces: a deployed, verified sheet; a branch summary; a PR.

Shell prelude, reused by every step (`REPO` quoted, since the path has spaces):

```bash
cd "/Users/bryanpreza/Documents/Visual Studio Code/Fantasy Basketball"
SHEET_ID="$(grep '^DRAFT_SHEET_ID=' .env | cut -d= -f2-)"
test -n "$SHEET_ID" || echo "STOP: DRAFT_SHEET_ID missing from .env"
mkdir -p /tmp/local-board-deploy data/draft-board/pulls
```

**Notes:**
- Step 3 records the ticks *before* the new `Build.gs` is deployed, so the live sheet has no
  `Data digest` row yet. `pull_sheet.py` plans that cell from the new layout and may refuse the
  pull on its cell count. Step 3 gives the fallback: three single-column CSV reads (names, MINE,
  GONE). gviz `tqx=out:csv` with the page's cookies answers this private sheet without a header
  (used live 2026-09-12); only `tqx=out:json` needs `X-DataSource-Auth: true`. CSV type-sniffs a
  column too, hence one column per read.
- A Full rebuild recreates Board and Settings: `buildDraftBoard` carries only the Draft Board's
  GONE, MINE and Notes across by name (`readCheckState`). Step 3b records every My GP override,
  XRank, GP Y-1/2/3, Board note and Settings input first, and stops for the owner if a rebuild
  would overwrite any. Step 5 diffs MINE, GONE and that record afterwards.
- Owner checkpoints are mandatory: a Step 3b record that would lose anything; before clicking
  **Allow** on Google's consent screen for every copied spreadsheet (Step 8.2, again on every
  fresh copy); and before `git push` / `gh pr create` (Step 12).
- The shell is zsh. Globs that may match nothing are avoided (Step 9 cleans up with `find`).
- `playwright-cli snapshot` and `screenshot` output lands in `.playwright-cli/` under the repo
  root, which is gitignored and holds sheet text and the sheet URL. Screenshots this task keeps
  are saved under `data/draft-board/` or `/tmp`, never anywhere else in the repo. Before each
  commit, `git status --short` shows neither.
- The helper scripts (`ticks_by_name.py`, `ticks_from_csv.py`, `hand_record.py`,
  `tie_order.py`, `swap_rows.py`, `push_buf.py`) live in `/tmp/local-board-deploy/` and are never
  committed. All but `push_buf.py` were run against synthetic pulls built from
  `tests/board_fixtures.py` while this plan was written; their browser calls were not.

- [ ] **Step 1: Confirm a clean branch and green gates, and fix the SHA to deploy.**

```bash
git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD            # local-draft-board
git status --porcelain                     # empty
pytest -q && ruff check . && (cd scripts/draft-board && node harness.js)
SHA="$(git rev-parse --short HEAD)"; echo "$SHA"
git show "$SHA:scripts/draft-board/Build.gs" > "/tmp/local-board-deploy/Build-$SHA.gs"
grep -c "9-Cat H2H Draft Board\|SETTINGS_DEFAULTS\|function stampData\|Data digest" "/tmp/local-board-deploy/Build-$SHA.gs"
```

  - **Verify:** branch name matches; porcelain is empty; all three gates exit 0; the grep count is
    ≥ 4.
  - **Stop if:** anything is uncommitted, a gate fails, or a marker is missing from the committed
    `Build.gs`. Fix and commit first; never deploy an uncommitted file.

- [ ] **Step 2: Build `Data.gs` and the snapshot, then verify them.**

```bash
python3 scripts/draft-board/build_data.py --dry-run
```

  - **Stop if:** it reports a missing file, mixed dates, or any "When it refuses to run" failure
    (build-and-maintenance.md). There is no newest complete set, so **stop and ask the owner**.
    Do not run `calibrate_bbm.py` or download anything.

```bash
python3 scripts/draft-board/build_data.py
(cd scripts/draft-board && node harness.js)
python3 scripts/draft-board/verify.py
DATE="$(ls data/draft-board/ | sed -n 's/^board - \(.*\)\.json$/\1/p' | sort | tail -1)"
python3 scripts/draft-board/verify.py \
  --published "data/player_data/BBM Published - BMP - $DATE.tsv" \
  --published "data/player_data/BBM Published - BMP-ALT - $DATE.tsv"
DIGEST="$(python3 -c "import sys,json; sys.path.insert(0,'scripts/draft-board'); import board_snapshot as B; print(B.load(B.newest(B.ROOT))['meta']['digest'])")"
echo "${DIGEST:0:12}"
```

  - **Verify:** `build_data.py` prints the snapshot path and a digest prefix equal to
    `${DIGEST:0:12}`; the harness passes on the real `Data.gs`; `verify.py` exits 0, including the
    snapshot ↔ `Data.gs` check; `--published` reports MAE around 0.003 with nothing outside display
    rounding.
  - **Stop if:** `verify.py` exits non-zero, or `--published` is off by more than display rounding.
    Do not deploy.

- [ ] **Step 3: Before deploying, record the live MINE ticks by name.**

Open the sheet in the signed-in profile:

```bash
playwright-cli -s=fantasy open --persistent "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/$SHEET_ID/edit"
playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "() => document.title"
```

  - **Stop if:** the title is a Google sign-in page. That means the session will not authenticate;
    stop and ask (AGENTS.md).

Write the tick reader to /tmp (tested on a synthetic pull: it reads any range split, pads nothing,
matches `v is True`):

```bash
cat > /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_by_name.py <<'EOF'
"""Names ticked MINE (or GONE) in a pull_sheet.py file. Usage: ticks_by_name.py PULL OUT [mine|drafted]"""
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def col_num(letters):
    n = 0
    for ch in letters:
        n = n * 26 + ord(ch) - 64
    return n


def column(pull, sheet, letter, first_row):
    want = col_num(letter)
    for r in pull["ranges"].values():
        m = re.fullmatch(r"([A-Z]+)(\d+):([A-Z]+)(\d+)", r["range"])
        if r["sheet"] != sheet or not m or int(m[2]) != first_row:
            continue
        if col_num(m[1]) <= want <= col_num(m[3]):
            return [row[want - col_num(m[1])] for row in r["cells"]]
    sys.exit(f"no {sheet} range in the pull covers column {letter} from row {first_row}")


layout = json.loads(Path("scripts/draft-board/board_layout.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
cols = layout["tabs"]["Draft Board"]["columns"]
pull = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
field = sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else "mine"
names = column(pull, "Draft Board", cols["player"]["letter"], layout["first_row"])
ticks = column(pull, "Draft Board", cols[field]["letter"], layout["first_row"])
ticked = sorted(n["v"] for n, t in zip(names, ticks, strict=True) if n and t and t["v"] is True)
Path(sys.argv[2]).write_text("".join(f"{n}\n" for n in ticked), encoding="utf-8")
print(f"{len(ticked)} ticked {field}")
EOF
python3 scripts/draft-board/pull_sheet.py --label live
PRE="$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*live.json | head -1)"
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_by_name.py "$PRE" data/draft-board/pulls/mine-before-deploy.txt mine
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_by_name.py "$PRE" data/draft-board/pulls/gone-before-deploy.txt drafted
```

**Fallback**, only if `pull_sheet.py` exits non-zero because the pre-deploy sheet lacks a range
the new layout plans (for example the `Data digest` cell). gviz `tqx=out:csv` answers this
private sheet with the page's cookies alone (only `tqx=out:json` needs the auth header), but it
type-sniffs a column exactly as JSON does, so names, MINE and GONE are three single-column reads:

```bash
cat > /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_from_csv.py <<'EOF'
"""The Step 3 fallback: names ticked MINE and GONE, from three single-type CSV reads.

Usage (from the repo root): playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "<the fetch above>" | ticks_from_csv.py
The eval returns JSON with CSV text under `names`, `mine` and `gone`, one column each, because
gviz type-sniffs a CSV column exactly as it does a JSON one.
"""
import csv
import io
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, "scripts/draft-board")

import pull_sheet  # noqa: E402

reply = pull_sheet.extract(sys.stdin.read())


def column(key):
    return [(row[0].strip() if row else "") for row in csv.reader(io.StringIO(reply[key]))]


names = column("names")
for key in ("mine", "gone"):
    ticked = sorted(n for n, t in zip(names, column(key), strict=False)
                    if n and t.upper() == "TRUE")
    Path(f"data/draft-board/pulls/{key}-before-deploy.txt").write_text(
        "".join(f"{n}\n" for n in ticked), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"{len(ticked)} ticked {key}")
EOF
read P M G <<< "$(python3 -c "import json; c = json.load(open('scripts/draft-board/board_layout.json'))['tabs']['Draft Board']['columns']; print(c['player']['letter'], c['mine']['letter'], c['drafted']['letter'])")"
playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "async () => { const u = r => 'https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/$SHEET_ID/gviz/tq?tqx=out:csv&sheet=Draft%20Board&headers=0&range=' + r; const g = r => fetch(u(r), {credentials: 'include'}).then(x => x.text()); return JSON.stringify({names: await g('${P}4:${P}203'), mine: await g('${M}4:${M}203'), gone: await g('${G}4:${G}203')}); }" \
  | python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_from_csv.py
```

  - **Verify:** `13 ticked mine` and `0 ticked gone` (the known live state), and
    `data/draft-board/pulls/mine-before-deploy.txt` holds 13 names. Keep the pull file if the
    primary route wrote one.
  - **Stop if:** the counts differ or both routes fail. The live sheet then contradicts what the
    plan assumes; stop and ask.

- [ ] **Step 3b: Record the Board's hand columns and the Settings inputs; stop if a Full rebuild would lose any.**

Step 5's Full rebuild recreates the Board and Settings tabs. `buildDraftBoard` carries only the
Draft Board's GONE, MINE and Notes across, by name (`readCheckState`). Every `My GP Est`
override, XRank, GP Y-1/2/3 and Board note, and every Settings input (`Sort by`, teams, roster,
tier multiplier, category band, disagreement gap, the win-rate cutoffs), is written fresh. So
record them first. Every range is one column, because gviz sniffs one type per column in JSON
and CSV alike.

```bash
cat > /tmp/local-board-deploy/hand_record.py <<'EOF'
"""The Board's hand columns and the Settings inputs, recorded around a Full rebuild.

Usage, from the repo root:
  hand_record.py pull LABEL [--route json|csv]
      pull them into data/draft-board/pulls/<UTC ts> LABEL.json and print the counts;
      exit 3 when a Full rebuild would lose something
  hand_record.py counts PULL.json               print the counts of a saved record again
  hand_record.py names PULL.json                the players behind the counts, terminal only
  hand_record.py compare PRE.json POST.json     Settings inputs and counts that changed

A Full rebuild recreates the Board and Settings tabs. It carries the Draft Board's GONE, MINE
and Notes across by name, and nothing else: My GP Est overrides, XRank, GP Y-1/2/3, Board
notes and every Settings input are written fresh. Every range is one column, so gviz has one
type per request to sniff; the CSV route needs no auth header, the JSON route does.
Output is counts and Settings keys; `names` prints player names and never writes them.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import io
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path.cwd()
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "scripts" / "draft-board"))

import board_settings as BSET  # noqa: E402
import pull_sheet as PS  # noqa: E402
import verify_local as VL  # noqa: E402

LAYOUT = json.loads((REPO / "scripts" / "draft-board" / "board_layout.json").read_text("utf-8"))
HAND = {"player": "string", "gp": "number", "myGp": "number", "gp1": "number",
        "gp2": "number", "gp3": "number", "xrank": "number", "notes": "string"}
INPUTS = {**dict.fromkeys((*BSET.SHEET_KEYS, "q"), "number"), "scoring": "string",
          "sort_by": "string"}
DEFAULT_SORT_LABEL = "BMP · DURH"
KEPT = ("my_gp_differs", "xrank", "gp1", "gp2", "gp3", "notes")


def plan(layout: dict = LAYOUT) -> list[dict]:
    first, last = layout["first_row"], layout["last_row"]
    cols, out = layout["tabs"]["Board"]["columns"], []
    for key, kind in HAND.items():
        c = cols[key]["index"]
        out.append(PS._range("Board", c, first, c, last, kind,
                             last - first + 1 if key == "player" else 0))
    for key, kind in INPUTS.items():
        c, r, _, _ = PS.parse_a1(layout["tabs"]["Settings"]["cells"][key])
        out.append(PS._range("Settings", c, r, c, r, kind, 1))
    return out


def csv_eval(sheet_id: str, ranges: list[dict]) -> str:
    base = f"https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/{sheet_id}/gviz/tq?tqx=out:csv&headers=0"
    todo = [{"name": r["name"], "sheet": r["sheet"], "range": r["range"]} for r in ranges]
    return ("async () => { const base = " + json.dumps(base) + "; const plan = "
            + json.dumps(todo, ensure_ascii=False) + "; const out = {}; "
            "for (const p of plan) { const u = base + '&sheet=' + encodeURIComponent(p.sheet) "
            "+ '&range=' + encodeURIComponent(p.range); "
            "out[p.name] = await fetch(u, {credentials: 'include'}).then(x => x.text()); } "
            "return JSON.stringify(out); }")


def from_csv(texts: dict, ranges: list[dict], label: str, pulled_at: str) -> dict:
    """CSV replies in the pull-file shape. CSV carries display text, so percentages are parsed."""
    out = {"pulled_at": pulled_at, "label": label, "ranges": {}}
    for r in ranges:
        rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(texts[r["name"]])))
        if len(rows) > r["rows"]:
            raise PS.PullError(f"{r['name']}: {len(rows)} rows returned for {r['rows']} planned")
        grid = [[None] for _ in range(r["rows"])]
        for i, row in enumerate(rows):
            text = row[0].strip() if row else ""
            if not text:
                continue
            if r["type"] == "number":
                try:
                    v = (float(text[:-1].replace(",", "")) / 100 if text.endswith("%")
                         else float(text.replace(",", "")))
                except ValueError:
                    v = None
                grid[i][0] = {"v": v, "f": text}
            else:
                grid[i][0] = {"v": text, "f": None}
        if sum(1 for g in grid if g[0] and g[0]["v"] is not None) < r["min_cells"]:
            raise PS.PullError(f"{r['name']}: fewer filled cells than {r['min_cells']}")
        out["ranges"][r["name"]] = {"sheet": r["sheet"], "range": r["range"],
                                    "type": r["type"], "cells": grid}
    return out


def measure(pull: dict, layout: dict = LAYOUT) -> tuple[dict, dict, dict]:
    """(counts, names per count, Settings inputs by key)."""
    sheet, cols = VL._Pull(pull), layout["tabs"]["Board"]["columns"]
    first, last = layout["first_row"], layout["last_row"]

    def cell(key, row):
        return sheet.cell("Board", cols[key]["index"], row)

    counts = dict.fromkeys(("board_rows", "my_gp_blank", *KEPT), 0)
    names = {k: [] for k in counts if k != "board_rows"}
    for row in range(first, last + 1):
        name = VL._text(cell("player", row))
        if not name:
            continue
        counts["board_rows"] += 1
        gp, my = VL._num(cell("gp", row)), VL._num(cell("myGp", row))
        hits = []
        if my is None:
            hits.append("my_gp_blank")
        elif gp is None or abs(my - gp) > 1e-9:
            hits.append("my_gp_differs")
        hits += [k for k in ("xrank", "gp1", "gp2", "gp3") if VL._v(cell(k, row)) is not None]
        if VL._text(cell("notes", row)):
            hits.append("notes")
        for k in hits:
            counts[k] += 1
            names[k].append(name)
    inputs = {}
    for key, kind in INPUTS.items():
        c = sheet.at("Settings", layout["tabs"]["Settings"]["cells"][key])
        inputs[key] = VL._text(c) if kind == "string" else VL._num(c)
    return counts, names, inputs


def drift(inputs: dict) -> list[str]:
    want = {**BSET.as_dict(), "sort_by": DEFAULT_SORT_LABEL}
    bad = []
    for key, kind in INPUTS.items():
        got = inputs[key]
        if kind == "string":
            same = got == want[key]
        else:
            same = got is not None and abs(got - want[key]) <= 1e-9
        if not same:
            bad.append(key)
    return bad


def report(pull: dict) -> int:
    counts, _, inputs = measure(pull)
    bad = drift(inputs)
    print("  ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in counts.items()))
    print("settings inputs differing from SETTINGS_DEFAULTS: " + (", ".join(bad) or "none"))
    if any(counts[k] for k in KEPT) or bad:
        print("STOP: a Full rebuild would overwrite these. Ask the owner before rebuilding.")
        return 3
    print("nothing a Full rebuild would lose" + (" (blank My GP rows are reseeded, not lost)"
                                                 if counts["my_gp_blank"] else ""))
    return 0


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,
                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    p = sub.add_parser("pull")
    p.add_argument("label")
    p.add_argument("--route", choices=("json", "csv"), default="json")
    for name in ("counts", "names"):
        sub.add_parser(name).add_argument("pull", type=Path)
    c = sub.add_parser("compare")
    c.add_argument("pre", type=Path)
    c.add_argument("post", type=Path)
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if args.cmd == "pull":
        ranges, sheet_id = plan(), PS.read_sheet_id(PS.ENV)
        fn = PS.build_eval(sheet_id, ranges) if args.route == "json" else csv_eval(sheet_id, ranges)
        proc = subprocess.run(["playwright-cli", "-s=fantasy", "eval", fn], cwd=REPO,
                              capture_output=True, text=True)
        if proc.returncode:
            print(proc.stderr.strip()[-600:], file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        now = datetime.now(UTC)
        stamp = now.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        try:
            raw = PS.extract(proc.stdout)
            pull = (PS.assemble(raw, ranges, args.label, stamp) if args.route == "json"
                    else from_csv(raw, ranges, args.label, stamp))
        except PS.PullError as exc:
            print(f"pull refused: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        out = REPO / "data" / "draft-board" / "pulls" / f"{now:%Y-%m-%dT%H%M%SZ} {args.label}.json"
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out.write_text(json.dumps(pull, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"wrote {out}")
        return report(pull)
    if args.cmd == "counts":
        return report(json.loads(args.pull.read_text("utf-8")))
    if args.cmd == "names":
        _, names, _ = measure(json.loads(args.pull.read_text("utf-8")))
        for key, who in names.items():
            for name in who:
                print(f"{key}\t{name}")
        return 0
    pre = measure(json.loads(args.pre.read_text("utf-8")))
    post = measure(json.loads(args.post.read_text("utf-8")))
    changed = [k for k in INPUTS if pre[2][k] != post[2][k]]
    moved = [k for k in pre[0] if pre[0][k] != post[0][k]]
    for k in changed:
        print(f"settings {k}: {pre[2][k]!r} -> {post[2][k]!r}")
    for k in moved:
        print(f"count {k}: {pre[0][k]} -> {post[0][k]}")
    print("no Settings input or hand-column count changed" if not changed and not moved else "")
    return 1 if changed or moved else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
EOF
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/hand_record.py pull pre-rebuild; echo "exit $?"
HAND_PRE="$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*pre-rebuild.json | head -1)"
```

over CSV, which this private sheet answers with the page's cookies alone:

```bash
HAND_PRE="$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*pre-rebuild.json | head -1)"
```

  - **Verify:** `exit 0` and `nothing a Full rebuild would lose`. The record stays under
    `data/draft-board/pulls/` for Step 5's comparison.
  - **Stop if:** `exit 3`. Run `python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/hand_record.py names "$HAND_PRE"`
    and tell the owner in chat which players carry an override, XRank, GP Y-1/2/3 or note, and

  - **Stop if:** both routes fail. A sheet that cannot be recorded cannot be rebuilt safely; ask.

- [ ] **Step 4: Deploy `Build.gs` into `Code.gs`, and `Data.gs`.**

Follow build-and-maintenance.md, "Pushing `Build.gs` into the sheet", with these specifics. Open
the bound project from the sheet (Extensions ▸ Apps Script) through `playwright-cli -s=fantasy
snapshot` and `click`. It opens a new tab, so run `playwright-cli -s=fantasy tab-list`, then
`tab-select` it. Write the chunk pusher, which calls `playwright-cli` with an argument list and so
avoids shell quoting of source text:

```bash
cat > /tmp/local-board-deploy/push_buf.py <<'EOF'
"""Load a file onto window.__buf in the selected playwright-cli tab, in 12KB chunks.

Usage: push_buf.py FILE. Prints the buffer length and the expected UTF-16 length; they must match.
"""
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path("/Users/bryanpreza/Documents/Visual Studio Code/Fantasy Basketball")
CHUNK = 12_000


def ev(fn):
    return subprocess.run(["playwright-cli", "-s=fantasy", "eval", fn], cwd=REPO, check=True,
                          capture_output=True, text=True).stdout


text = Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
ev("() => { window.__buf = ''; return 0; }")
for i in range(0, len(text), CHUNK):
    ev("() => { window.__buf += " + json.dumps(text[i:i + CHUNK]) + "; return window.__buf.length; }")
print(ev("() => window.__buf.length"))
print("expected", len(text.encode("utf-16-le")) // 2, "lines", text.count("\n") + 1)
EOF
```

**4a — Code.gs.** Record the model before, push, apply, verify, save, reload, verify again:

```bash
playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "() => { const m = monaco.editor.getModels().find(x => x.getValue().indexOf('9-Cat H2H Draft Board') !== -1); return m ? [m.getLineCount(), m.getValue().length] : 'NO MODEL'; }"
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/push_buf.py "/tmp/local-board-deploy/Build-$SHA.gs"
playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "() => { const m = monaco.editor.getModels().find(x => x.getValue().indexOf('9-Cat H2H Draft Board') !== -1); m.setValue(window.__buf); const v = m.getValue(); return [m.getLineCount(), v.length, ['SETTINGS_DEFAULTS', 'function stampData', 'Data digest'].map(s => v.indexOf(s) !== -1)]; }"
playwright-cli -s=fantasy press Meta+s
playwright-cli -s=fantasy reload
playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "() => { const m = monaco.editor.getModels().find(x => x.getValue().indexOf('9-Cat H2H Draft Board') !== -1); const v = m.getValue(); return [m.getLineCount(), v.length, ['SETTINGS_DEFAULTS', 'function stampData', 'Data digest'].map(s => v.indexOf(s) !== -1)]; }"
```

  - **Verify:** the buffer length printed equals `expected`. Both after-apply and after-reload
    return the `lines` count from `push_buf.py`, the same length, and `[true, true, true]`.
    `Code.gs` must be in the editor's file list; open it first if the model is not loaded
    (`NO MODEL`).
  - **Stop if:** any count differs or any marker is `false` after the reload. Do not run a menu
    action. Re-push; if it fails twice, stop and ask.

**4b — Data.gs.** Same sequence, identifying the model by
`'GENERATED by scripts/draft-board/build_data.py'` and checking for the snapshot digest:

```bash

playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "() => { const m = monaco.editor.getModels().find(x => x.getValue().indexOf('GENERATED by scripts/draft-board/build_data.py') !== -1); m.setValue(window.__buf); const v = m.getValue(); return [m.getLineCount(), v.length, v.indexOf('$DIGEST') !== -1]; }"

```

- [ ] **Step 5: Full rebuild, confirm the ticks, refresh, re-sort, read the sanity block.**

`tab-select` the sheet tab, then `playwright-cli -s=fantasy reload` so the menu loads the new code.
`snapshot`, `click` the **Draft Board** menu, `snapshot`, `click` **Full rebuild (from Data.gs)**.
Never a `Step N` item (AGENTS.md). If an authorization dialog appears, stop and ask; the scopes
should not have changed. The run takes up to six minutes; `snapshot` every 30 seconds until the
completion toast or an error. A full rebuild leaves **README** active, so click the
**Draft Board** tab before any positional read.

```bash
python3 scripts/draft-board/pull_sheet.py --label live
POST="$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*live.json | head -1)"
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_by_name.py "$POST" data/draft-board/pulls/mine-after-rebuild.txt mine
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_by_name.py "$POST" data/draft-board/pulls/gone-after-rebuild.txt drafted
diff data/draft-board/pulls/mine-before-deploy.txt data/draft-board/pulls/mine-after-rebuild.txt && echo "mine survived"
diff data/draft-board/pulls/gone-before-deploy.txt data/draft-board/pulls/gone-after-rebuild.txt && echo "gone survived"
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/hand_record.py pull post-rebuild
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/hand_record.py compare "$HAND_PRE" "$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*post-rebuild.json | head -1)"; echo "exit $?"
```

(If the JSON route refuses the post-rebuild record, add `--route csv`, as in Step 3b.)

  - **Verify:** `mine survived`, `gone survived`, and the comparison prints `no Settings input or
    hand-column count changed` and `exit 0`.
  - **If a MINE or GONE tick did not survive:** for each name only in the `-before-deploy.txt`
    file, find its sheet row: its index in the pull's `player` column plus `first_row`. The MINE
    or GONE letter comes from the layout (`mine`, `drafted`). Type that A1 address into the Name
    Box (`snapshot`, `click` the Name Box ref, `type`, `press Enter`), then `press Space` to tick
    it. Pull and diff again until both survive.
  - **Stop if:** a name cannot be found on the rebuilt board, or the comparison exits 1 with a
    change the owner did not accept at Step 3b. Ask; never tick a guessed player or re-type a
    value from memory.

Then, from the **Draft Board** menu: **Refresh data (new export, keeps your edits)**, wait for the
toast, then **Rebuild & re-sort**, wait. If the refresh toast reports a reorder, re-run the pull
and diff above (I1: ticks can misattach on a reorder refresh). Click the **Settings** tab and
`playwright-cli -s=fantasy snapshot`.

  - **Verify:** `Names line up across tabs` and `Draft Board rows line up` both read `aligned`;
    `Data digest` equals `$DIGEST`; `Data generated` equals `$DATE`.
  - **Stop if:** either reads MISALIGNED or the digest differs. Do not proceed to verification.

- [ ] **Step 6: Verify the local engine against the live sheet.**

```bash
python3 scripts/draft-board/pull_sheet.py --label live
LIVE="$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*live.json | head -1)"
python3 scripts/draft-board/verify.py --local "$LIVE"; echo "exit $?"
```

  - **Verify:** `exit 0`. Keep the aggregate counts it prints for the branch summary.
  - **On exit 3** (not comparable): the sheet is not running this `Build.gs`, or the digest
    differs. Return to Step 4 or 5.
  - **On exit 1** (mismatch), apply the triage rule. If the sheet evaluates differently from
    `Build.gs`'s formula text (an array trap), fix `Build.gs`. If the engine differs from a
    correctly evaluating sheet, fix `board_engine.py`. **Never widen a tolerance.** Write it up
    in `docs/bugs/2026-09-12-<slug>.md`, commit the fix with tests, re-run Step 1, redeploy
    (Step 4) if `Build.gs` changed, and repeat this step until exit 0.

- [ ] **Step 6b: Record whether the sheet breaks ties the engine's way.**

`verify.py --local` compares tied rows as sets, in rank and in displayed order, on the `#`
column and in every Punts block. This records whether the live sheet actually followed the
engine's tie-break (Board row order) on this pull. It is evidence for a later change, not a
change on this branch.

```bash
cat > /tmp/local-board-deploy/tie_order.py <<'EOF'
"""Whether the sheet breaks ties the way the engine does, on one pull. Aggregate output only.

Usage (from the repo root): tie_order.py PULL.json SNAPSHOT.json

verify.py --local compares tied rows as sets: which of two equal values the sheet shows
first, and which rank it gives each, is not assumed to follow the engine's tie-break on Board
row. This reports whether it did on this pull, for the Draft Board's # and for every Punts
block that has a tie. It prints counts, never names.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, "scripts/draft-board")

import board_engine as ENGINE  # noqa: E402
import board_snapshot as BS  # noqa: E402
import verify_local as VL  # noqa: E402

snapshot = BS.load(Path(sys.argv[2]))
pulls = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
layout = json.loads(VL.LAYOUT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
sheet = VL._Pull(pulls)
inputs = VL._engine_inputs(snapshot, sheet, layout)
applied = VL._applied_sort(snapshot, sheet, layout, inputs["order"], VL._Report())
if applied is None:
    sys.exit("the pull shows none of the snapshot's value columns; run verify.py --local first")
players, first = snapshot["players"], layout["first_row"]
order, engine = inputs["order"], ENGINE.order(snapshot, applied)
src, kind = applied["source"], applied["kind"]
vals = [players[i]["values"][src][kind]["v"] for i in order]
tied = [i for i, v in enumerate(vals) if vals.count(v) > 1]
dcols = layout["tabs"]["Draft Board"]["columns"]
state = inputs["state"]
state["applied_sort"] = dict(applied)
engine_rank = {r["key"]: r["rank"]
               for r in ENGINE.board_rows(snapshot, state, inputs["settings"], applied)}
order_same = sum(1 for i in tied if order[i] == engine[i])
rank_same = sum(1 for i in tied
                if VL._num(sheet.cell("Draft Board", dcols["rank"]["index"], first + i))
                == engine_rank[players[order[i]]["key"]])

p = layout["tabs"]["Punts"]
full = ENGINE.punts(snapshot, top=p["rows"])
blocks_tied = blocks_same = 0
for b in p["blocks"]:
    want = full[b["key"]]
    keys = [-1e9 if e["gap"] is None else e["gap"] for e in want]
    if len(set(keys)) == len(keys):
        continue
    blocks_tied += 1
    col = b["first_col"] + p["columns"]["name"]["offset"]
    got = [VL._text(sheet.cell("Punts", col, p["first_row"] + j)) for j in range(p["rows"])]
    blocks_same += [g for g in got if g] == [e["name"] for e in want]

print(f"Draft Board: {len(tied)} tied rows; {order_same} shown in the engine's order; "
      f"{rank_same} with the engine's #")
print(f"Punts: {blocks_tied} blocks with a tie; {blocks_same} shown in the engine's order")
if not tied and not blocks_tied:
    print("VERDICT: NOT PROVEN -- this pull has no ties")
elif order_same == rank_same == len(tied) and blocks_same == blocks_tied:
    print("VERDICT: EQUAL -- every tie on this pull follows the engine's tie-break")
else:
    print("VERDICT: DIFFERS -- keep comparing tie groups as sets")
EOF
python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/tie_order.py "$LIVE" "data/draft-board/board - $DATE.json"
```

  - **Verify:** two count lines and one `VERDICT:` line print; keep all three for the branch
    summary. Only counts are printed.
  - **If the verdict is `EQUAL`:** Step 10 records the follow-up. `NOT PROVEN` and `DIFFERS`
    leave the set comparison as the rule. Either way `verify_local.py` is not changed on this
    branch.

- [ ] **Step 7: Look at the board.**

On the Draft Board tab, then on the Category Tracker tab (`click` the tab ref each time):

```bash
playwright-cli -s=fantasy screenshot --filename=data/draft-board/pulls/draft-board-live.png
playwright-cli -s=fantasy screenshot --filename=data/draft-board/pulls/category-tracker-live.png
```

Open both PNGs with the Read tool and look at them.

  - **Verify:** no `#VALUE!`, `#REF!`, `#ERROR!` or `#NUM!`; tier breaks drawn; MINE rows shaded;
    the tracker's eight rows carry a Win % and a read. With 13 MINE, none is blank.
  - **Stop if:** anything reads wrong on sight even though Step 6 passed. Screenshots have caught
    defects the values missed; investigate before continuing.

- [ ] **Step 8: Tick scenario on a copy.**

Follow the tick-scenario procedure Task 8 added to build-and-maintenance.md (spec §4, "Tick
scenario"), with these specifics. First write the row picker that sub-step 3e uses:

```bash
cat > /tmp/local-board-deploy/swap_rows.py <<'EOF'
"""Three clean Board rows on a copy pull, for tick-scenario step e: two to swap, one to override.

Usage (from the repo root): swap_rows.py PULL.json
Prints ROW_A, NAME_A, ROW_B, NAME_B, OVERRIDE_ROW, tab-separated, to the terminal only.

Clean means no GONE or MINE tick, no note, no XRank, no GP Y-1/2/3 and My GP equal to projected
GP. The Draft Board points at fixed Board rows (I1), so a swapped name beside a tick would carry
the tick onto another player during the refresh, and verify.py --local, which reads ticks back
as truth, would still pass. Rows are taken from the bottom of the Board, the furthest from any
pick.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, "scripts/draft-board")

import verify_local as VL  # noqa: E402

layout = json.loads(VL.LAYOUT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
sheet = VL._Pull(json.loads(Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
first, last = layout["first_row"], layout["last_row"]
d, b = layout["tabs"]["Draft Board"]["columns"], layout["tabs"]["Board"]["columns"]


def draft(key, row):
    return sheet.cell("Draft Board", d[key]["index"], row)


def board(key, row):
    return sheet.cell("Board", b[key]["index"], row)


touched = set()
for row in range(first, last + 1):
    name = VL._text(draft("player", row))
    if not name:
        continue
    gp, my = VL._num(draft("projGp", row)), VL._num(draft("myGp", row))
    if (VL._v(draft("drafted", row)) is True or VL._v(draft("mine", row)) is True
            or VL._text(draft("notes", row)) or VL._v(draft("xrank", row)) is not None
            or gp is None or my is None or abs(gp - my) > 1e-9):
        touched.add(name)

clean = []
for row in range(first, last + 1):
    name = VL._text(board("player", row))
    if (not name or name in touched or VL._text(board("notes", row))
            or any(VL._v(board(k, row)) is not None for k in ("gp1", "gp2", "gp3", "xrank"))):
        continue
    clean.append((row, name))
if len(clean) < 3:
    sys.exit("STOP: fewer than three clean Board rows on this copy")
(row_a, name_a), (row_b, name_b), (override_row, _) = clean[-1], clean[-2], clean[-3]
print(f"{row_a}\t{name_a}\t{row_b}\t{name_b}\t{override_row}")
EOF
```

1. **Make the copy.** In the live sheet: `snapshot`, `click` File ▸ Make a copy, confirm. The
   copy opens in a new tab; `tab-list`, `tab-select` it, then:

   ```bash
   COPY_ID="$(playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "() => JSON.stringify({v: location.pathname.split('/')[3]})" | python3 -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'scripts/draft-board'); import pull_sheet; print(pull_sheet.extract(sys.stdin.read())['v'])")"
   COPY_TITLE="$(playwright-cli -s=fantasy eval "() => JSON.stringify({v: document.title.replace(/ - Google Sheets$/, '')})" | python3 -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'scripts/draft-board'); import pull_sheet; print(pull_sheet.extract(sys.stdin.read())['v'])")"
   case "$COPY_TITLE" in "Copy of "*) echo "on the copy" ;; *) echo "STOP: this tab is not the copy" ;; esac
   ```

   Never write `COPY_ID` or `COPY_TITLE` to a file in the repo.

2. **Authorise the copy's script.** Run **Draft Board ▸ Rebuild & re-sort** once on the copy.
   The consent screen asks for access to all your Google Sheets, the screen the 2026-09-01
   deploy declined. The spec directs granting it for the copy. **Confirm with the owner
   in-session before clicking Allow.** If the owner declines, or the flow cannot complete, stop
   and ask how to proceed.

   **2b. Clear the ticks the copy inherited.** The copy starts with the live sheet's MINE, GONE
   and Punted ticks, and the scenario's counts only mean something from zero.

   ```bash
   read GL ML PL R0T <<< "$(python3 -c "import json, sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'scripts/draft-board'); import pull_sheet as P; L = json.load(open('scripts/draft-board/board_layout.json')); d = L['tabs']['Draft Board']['columns']; t = L['tabs']['Category Tracker']; print(d['drafted']['letter'], d['mine']['letter'], P.col_letter(t['columns']['punted']), t['first_cat_row'])")"
   echo "Draft Board ${GL}4:${ML}203   Category Tracker ${PL}${R0T}:${PL}$((R0T + 7))"
   ```

   On the copy's **Draft Board** tab: `snapshot`, `click` the Name Box, `type` the Draft Board
   range printed above, `press Enter`, `press Delete` (a checkbox cleared with Delete reads
   unticked and keeps its checkbox). On the **Category Tracker** tab, the same with the tracker
   range. Then:

   ```bash
   python3 scripts/draft-board/pull_sheet.py --sheet-id "$COPY_ID" --label copy
   COPY_PULL="$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*copy.json | head -1)"
   python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_by_name.py "$COPY_PULL" /tmp/local-board-deploy/copy-mine.txt mine
   python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/ticks_by_name.py "$COPY_PULL" /tmp/local-board-deploy/copy-gone.txt drafted
   python3 -c "import json, sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'scripts/draft-board'); import verify_local as V; L = json.loads(V.LAYOUT.read_text()); p = V._Pull(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))); t = L['tabs']['Category Tracker']; print(sum(V._v(p.cell('Category Tracker', t['columns']['punted'], t['first_cat_row'] + i)) is True for i in range(8)), 'punted')" "$COPY_PULL"
   python3 scripts/draft-board/verify.py --local "$COPY_PULL"; echo "exit $?"
   ```

   - **Verify:** `0 ticked mine`, `0 ticked drafted`, `0 punted` and `exit 0`.

3. **Run the scenario.** After **each** sub-step below (and after each MINE count in a):

   ```bash
   python3 scripts/draft-board/pull_sheet.py --sheet-id "$COPY_ID" --label copy
   COPY_PULL="$(ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*copy.json | head -1)"
   python3 scripts/draft-board/verify.py --local "$COPY_PULL"; echo "exit $?"
   ```

   a. MINE counts 0, then 8, then 14. Select the MINE cells through the Name Box (a range of
      checkbox cells, then `press Space`) — this hits the `min(q, teams × n)` cap.
   b. GONE on about ten rows spread over tiers and positions, including two MINE rows.
   c. Category Tracker Punted: one category, then two (letter and rows as in 2b).
   d. Change `Sort by` (Draft Board row 1 dropdown) to another `SOURCE · KIND` label, then
      **Rebuild & re-sort**.
   e. **A reordering refresh on clean rows (the C1 check).** Pick the rows from the pull taken
      after d:

      ```bash
      IFS=$'\t' read -r SWAP_A NAME_A SWAP_B NAME_B OVERRIDE_ROW <<< "$(python3 /tmp/local-board-deploy/swap_rows.py "$COPY_PULL")"
      read BP BMY <<< "$(python3 -c "import json; c = json.load(open('scripts/draft-board/board_layout.json'))['tabs']['Board']['columns']; print(c['player']['letter'], c['myGp']['letter'])")"
      echo "swap Board rows $SWAP_A and $SWAP_B; override My GP on Board row $OVERRIDE_ROW"
      ```

      Record the three row numbers for the branch summary; the names stay in the shell. The
      three rows carry no GONE or MINE tick, no note, no XRank or GP Y-1/2/3, and My GP equal to
      projected GP. That is deliberate. The Draft Board points at fixed Board rows (I1), so a
      swapped name beside a tick would carry the tick onto another player during the refresh,
      and `verify.py --local`, which reads ticks back as truth, would still pass on a board whose
      ticks had moved. Clean rows make a pass mean C1 is fixed, not that I1 happened to agree.

      On the copy's **Board** tab, through the Name Box: go to `${BMY}${OVERRIDE_ROW}`, type
      `50`, Enter; go to `${BP}${SWAP_A}`, type `$NAME_B`, Enter; go to `${BP}${SWAP_B}`, type
      `$NAME_A`, Enter. Run **Draft Board ▸ Refresh data** and `snapshot` its toast: it must
      report hand edits re-attached (the reordering path), not an in-place update.
      `refreshWithReorder` writes the Board back in `Data.gs` order, so the digest is unchanged
      and the pull stays comparable. After the pull and verify above, confirm the override
      stayed on its row:

      ```bash
      python3 -c "import json, sys; sys.path.insert(0, 'scripts/draft-board'); import verify_local as V; L = json.loads(V.LAYOUT.read_text()); p = V._Pull(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))); print(V._num(p.cell('Board', L['tabs']['Board']['columns']['myGp']['index'], int(sys.argv[2]))))" "$COPY_PULL" "$OVERRIDE_ROW"
      ```

      `verify.py --local` fails on the Draft Board `My GP` column if any row without the
      override came back blank.

   - **Verify:** every `verify.py --local` run prints `exit 0`, and the last command prints
     `50.0`.

4. **Delete the copy**, found by its exact title, and nothing else:

   ```bash
   Q="$(python3 -c "import sys, urllib.parse; print(urllib.parse.quote('title:\"' + sys.argv[1] + '\"'))" "$COPY_TITLE")"
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy goto "https://drive.google.com/drive/search?q=$Q"
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy snapshot
   ```

   The results must hold exactly one file named exactly `$COPY_TITLE`; if there are more, or the
   match lacks the `Copy of ` prefix, stop and ask. `click` that one file to select it,
   `snapshot`, `click` the toolbar's **Move to trash**, confirm. Then
   `playwright-cli -s=fantasy goto "https://drive.google.com/drive/trash"`, `snapshot`, `click`
   the single row named exactly `$COPY_TITLE` to select only it, `snapshot`, `click` the
   toolbar's **Delete forever**, and confirm. **Never use Empty trash**: the owner's trash may
   hold files that are not this copy.

   ```bash
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy goto "https://drive.google.com/drive/search?q=$Q"
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy snapshot
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy goto "https://drive.google.com/drive/trash"
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy snapshot
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy goto "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/$COPY_ID/edit"
   playwright-cli -s=fantasy snapshot
   ```

   - **Verify:** the search finds no file with that title, Trash does not list it, and the
     copy's URL shows a not-found or no-access page. `tab-select` the live sheet afterwards.
   - **Stop if:** the copy still opens or still appears. Do not leave provider data in Drive;
     retry the single-item deletion, then ask.

**If a verify in 2b or 3 fails**, in this order:

1. Keep every pull. Apply the Step 6 triage rule and write it up in `docs/bugs/`.
2. Delete the failed copy exactly as in 8.4: search by exact title, Move to trash, then Delete
   forever on that single item.
3. If the fix is in `board_engine.py` only (the sheet's build and digest unchanged), commit it
   with its tests, then re-run `verify.py --local` against every cached scenario pull
   (`ls -t data/draft-board/pulls/*copy.json`) before touching Drive again; each must exit 0.
4. If the fix is in `Build.gs`, commit it with its tests, re-run Step 1 for the new SHA,
   redeploy `Code.gs` to the **live** sheet (Step 4a), and pass Step 3b, Step 5 and Step 6 there
   again.
5. Make a fresh copy (8.1). It is a new Apps Script project, so authorise it again (8.2) and
   **ask the owner before clicking Allow**, as before.
6. Resume the scenario from 8.2b.

- [ ] **Step 9: Exercise `board.py` against the real snapshot, in a practice state.**

A practice state path keeps any real `draft-state.json` untouched:

`--state` takes a relative path under `data/draft-board/`, so the practice state is named by
its file name alone (`data/draft-board/practice-state.json` would resolve to
`data/draft-board/data/draft-board/…`):

```bash
PRACTICE=practice-state.json
test ! -e "data/draft-board/$PRACTICE" || echo "STOP: a practice state already exists"
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" new
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" status
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" board --top 10
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" tracker
```

Take one name from the `board --top 10` output (in the shell only, never in a file):

```bash
NAME="<copy one player name from the board --top 10 output above>"
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" player "$NAME"
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" mine "$NAME" --pick 1 --show tracker
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" undo
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" tracker
python3 scripts/draft-board/board.py --state "$PRACTICE" check; echo "exit $?"
find data/draft-board -maxdepth 1 -name 'practice-state*' -delete
ls data/draft-board | grep -c practice-state
```

(`find` rather than `rm` with a glob: the Bash tool runs zsh, where an unmatched
`practice-state.*.bak.json` aborts the whole `rm` and leaves the practice state behind.)

  - **Verify:** `status` shows the pin equal to `$DATE` and `${DIGEST:0:12}`; `mine` echoes the
    name, team and rank; after `undo`, `tracker` shows 0 players ticked; `check` prints `exit 0`;
    the final `grep -c` prints `0`.
  - **Stop if:** any command exits non-zero other than by design, or `check` fails. File it under
    the Step 6 triage rule.

```markdown
- Branch: `local-draft-board` (N commits — fill from `git rev-list --count main..HEAD` run now, plus 1 for this summary's own commit)

## In one paragraph
The board now exists twice: the Google Sheet a person drafts from, and a local JSON snapshot
with a Python mirror of the sheet's draft-day formulas that an agent reads and ticks through
`board.py`. One `build_data.py` run writes both; they are kept in step by applying the same
change to both, never by pulling one from the other; `verify.py --local` holds the engine to
the live sheet.

## Why
The six owner requirements and where each is met — the table from the design spec §0,
condensed to one line each.

## What was built
Table: file | what it does — `board_settings.py`, `board_snapshot.py`, `board_engine.py`,
`board_names.py`, `board_state.py`, `board.py`, `pull_sheet.py`, `board_layout.json`, the
`build_data.py` / `verify.py` / `export_yahoo_rankings.py` / `review_mock_draft.py` / `Build.gs` /
`harness.js` / `check-no-data.sh` changes (design spec §5.1).
Tests: N passed — fill from the summary line of Step 1 `pytest -q` output.

## Decisions
- Approach A, and the two rejected (ADR-0022).
- Win-rate cutoffs 40/60/75 on both boards, and the at-target cost stated plainly (ADR-0023,
  superseding ADR-0018 in part).
- Mock-draft replay deferred with the `review_mock_draft.py` port.
- Review findings declined — one line pointing to the design spec §7.

## Live-sheet defects found during design
The §6 table: C1 fixed (a reorder refresh blanked unoverridden My GP); I1 written up in
docs/bugs, fix deferred; "Data generated" stale after Refresh, fixed by the stamp; the SORT
STALE claim removed; the cheat sheet's GONE-benchmark claim corrected; tracker raw totals
always HBP, documented.

## Deployment record
- Deployed `Build.gs` from commit SHA — fill from Step 1 `echo "$SHA"` output.
- `Code.gs` line count before → after — fill from Step 4a first and last eval outputs.
- `Data.gs` pushed, digest prefix — fill from Step 2 `echo "${DIGEST:0:12}"` output; projections dated — fill from Step 2 `$DATE`.
- MINE and GONE ticks recorded before deploy: counts — fill from Step 3 printed counts; both survived the Full rebuild: yes/no and how many re-ticked — fill from Step 5 diff results.
- Board hand columns and Settings inputs before the rebuild: the count line and the differing-keys line — fill from Step 3b output; unchanged after it — fill from Step 5 `compare` output.
- Menu actions run, in order: Full rebuild, Refresh data, Rebuild & re-sort.
- Browser artefacts: `playwright-cli` snapshots and screenshots are in the gitignored `.playwright-cli/` (they hold sheet text and the sheet URL) and under `data/draft-board/pulls/`; none anywhere else in the repo.

### Verified in the sheet
- Settings sanity: both alignment rows and the digest match — fill from Step 5 snapshot reading.
- `verify.py --local` against the live sheet: exit code and aggregate counts — fill from Step 6 output (counts only, no names).
- Tie order on the live pull: the two count lines and the verdict — fill from Step 6b output.
- Screenshots of the Draft Board and Category Tracker looked at; what was checked — fill from Step 7 observations.
- Tick scenario on a copy: ticks cleared to zero, then each of the five steps and its exit code — fill from Step 8.2b and 8.3 outputs; the Board rows swapped and overridden — fill from Step 8.3e echo; failed copies and their fixes, or none — fill from the Step 8 failure loop; copy deleted by exact title and confirmed — fill from Step 8.4.
- `board.py` practice run: commands run and `check` exit code — fill from Step 9 output; practice state removed.

## Known gaps
Verbatim from design spec §4: conditional-format bindings and colours; the disagreement gap
baked into rules at build time; I1 tick misattachment (ticks read back are taken as truth); a
My GP override indistinguishable from its seed formula in a values pull.
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Close C1 in the bug record and commit the summary.**

Step 8.3e is the in-sheet check the C1 write-up was waiting on, and it has passed (this step is
not reachable otherwise). Record that in the bug write-up with exact replacements:

```bash
python3 - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path

path = Path("docs/bugs/2026-09-12-reorder-refresh-tick-misattachment.md")
text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
edits = [
    ("  **C1 fixed** in the commit that adds this file (see [the second defect]"
     "(#c1-the-same-refresh-blanked-my-gp--fixed)).\n"
     "  Not yet deployed to the sheet.\n",
     "  **C1 fixed** in the commit that adds this file (see [the second defect]"
     "(#c1-the-same-refresh-blanked-my-gp--fixed)),\n"
     "  deployed, and verified in the sheet by tick-scenario step 5 on a copy.\n"),
    ("- **In the sheet: not yet.** The harness takes on trust that `setValues` stores a string\n"
     "  beginning with `=` as a formula, which only Sheets can confirm. It is step 5 of the tick\n"
     "  scenario (a My GP override followed by a reordering `Refresh data`, on a copy), and this\n"
     "  defect is not closed until that step passes.\n",
     "- **In the sheet: verified.** Tick-scenario step 5 ran on a copy of the live sheet: a My GP\n"
     "  override on one clean Board row, two other clean rows' names swapped to force the\n"
     "  reordering `Refresh data`, then `pull_sheet.py` and `verify.py --local`, which exited 0.\n"
     "  Every row without the override came back on its seeding formula and the override stayed\n"
     "  on its row, so `setValues` does store a string beginning with `=` as a formula. C1 is\n"
     "  closed; I1 is not.\n"),
]
for old, new in edits:
    assert text.count(old) == 1, old[:60]
    text = text.replace(old, new)
path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
print("C1 closed in", path)
EOF
pytest -q && ruff check . && (cd scripts/draft-board && node harness.js)
git add docs/project-updates/2026-09-12-local-draft-board-branch-summary.md \
  docs/bugs/2026-09-12-reorder-refresh-tick-misattachment.md
git status --short     # only those two files; nothing under data/ or .playwright-cli/
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
docs: record the local-draft-board branch, deployed and verified live

What the branch built, the decisions behind it, what the deploy did to the live
sheet, and how it was verified there: the hand-edit record before the Full

The C1 write-up is closed in the sheet: the tick scenario forced a reordering
refresh on clean rows of a copy, and verify.py --local passed.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
```

## Self-Review

### Spec coverage

| Spec item | Requirement | Task and step |
|---|---|---|
| §0.1 | Format chosen for agent use | Task 4 Steps 3 and 5 (compact named JSON, never pretty-printed); Task 6 (read only through `board.py`) |
| §0.2 | Never committed or pushed; CLAUDE.md says so | Task 1 Step 3 (guard patterns); Task 4 Step 11 (AGENTS.md boundaries) |
| §0.3 | The sheet's dynamic calculations run locally | Task 5 Steps 4, 8, 12, 16, 20 |
| §0.4 | Kept in sync by applying the same update to both, never by pulling | Task 4 Step 5 (one run writes both, one digest), Step 11 (sync boundary); Task 7 (layout staleness); Task 8 Steps 7–10 (parity check, change procedure) |
| §0.5 | More efficient for an agent than Playwright | Task 6 (one local call, TSV/JSON, `--show` on writes) |

The ADR text stands as drafted |

### Reconciliation changes

1. **One snapshot builder.** `tests/board_fixtures.py` is created in Task 5 Step 1 and imported by Tasks 5, 6, 8 and 9. It keeps Task 6's shape (15 rows, teams 3 / roster 4 / q 12 / gap 3, team, position, ADP and injury cycles, strictly falling values, sealed digest, `write_snapshot`) and adds Task 5's `set_values` and `tick`, Task 5's dropped-category walk (so the tag test's labels hold), `k_tracker` 1.0 and `k_rosenof` in DERIV, and `q` rescaling when a `settings` override changes `teams` or `roster`. Settings derive from `board_settings.as_dict()` instead of a transcribed copy. Why: the two drafts' builders disagreed on settings, positions, ADP, `hbp_raw`, `k_tracker` and the digest, and the brief required one.
2. **Task 5 engine tests restated against the shared builder.** `make_snapshot(n, teams=…)` → `settings={…}` at five call sites; the tag test passes `settings={"disagree_gap": 15}`; the gap test sets row 1's ADP; the strengths test zeroes every `d` block; the zero-attempts test sets `ftm`/`fta` on every row; the tracker roster reads `pos` from the snapshot; the checks test sets every ADP and reads the digest from the snapshot. Why: each assertion had relied on a default of Task 5's private builder. Stage counts are unchanged (8, 15, 28, 36, 41) and were reproduced.
3. **Task 6 no longer creates the builder.** Its Step 1 is removed, its steps renumbered 1–24, and its Files, Interfaces, Notes, `git add` and "seven paths" updated. Why: follows from 1.
4. **Task 8's `verify.py` edits rewritten on top of Task 4.** Three edits (docstring pair, import, `--snapshot` + `--local` + dispatch) replace four anchored on the pre-Task-4 file; the `--local` branch honours `--snapshot` and returns before `Data.gs` is read; `--snapshot`'s help documents both defaults. Why: the drafts edited `verify.py` independently.
5. **Task 8 now adds the AGENTS.md draft-day pointer and the rebase condition.** New Edit 3 of 3 in Step 11, and the commit body says so. Why: Task 6 deferred both to Task 8, and Task 8's draft did neither (spec §5.3).
6. **Task 8 procedure wording.** Step 10 Edit 3 says `verify.py` checks the snapshot dated `META.generated`, not "the newest". Why: that is what Task 4's code does (Task 4's flagged deviation).
7. **Tick-scenario step 5 made executable.** Task 8 Step 10 (Edit 7) and Task 12 Step 8e force the reordering refresh by swapping two Board `Player` cells on the copy. Why: with `Data.gs` unchanged, `refreshData` takes the in-place path and the C1 fix would never be exercised; `refreshData` decides the path by comparing Board names with `PLAYERS` (checked in `Build.gs`).
8. **Task 9 keeps `import board_settings as BSET`.** Why: its draft replaced the import with an unaliased one, which breaks Task 2's `BSET.SEASON` and `BSET.Q`.
9. **Task 9 expected counts corrected** to the replayed run: red `1 failed, 25 passed, 10 errors`, green `36 passed` (24 existing + 12). Why: the draft said 20 existing and gave two different green counts.
10. **Task 11 uses the real names.** The scripts README gains a `verify_local.py` row, and the test paragraph becomes a table of every new test file plus `tests/board_fixtures.py`; the "ls and rename" hedge and the "merge the intent" hedge are replaced by checked statements. The route evaluation's addendum notes the refresh procedure grew from twelve to fourteen steps. Why: the draft guessed `tests/test_board.py`.
11. **Task 12 Step 9 practice state.** `--state practice-state.json` instead of `data/draft-board/practice-state.json`, and cleanup by `find … -delete`. Why: a relative `--state` resolves under `data/draft-board/`, and zsh aborts `rm` on an unmatched `*.bak.json` glob (both reproduced in scratch).
12. **Task 4 Step 4 pins In pool.** `test_settings_are_board_settings` asserts `snapshot["settings"]["q"] == snapshot["deriv"]["q"]`. Why: the engine reads `settings["q"]`, the sheet `DERIV.q`.
13. **Task 4 prose made definite.** No conditional about Task 2 touching `tests/test_build_data.py` (it does not), and the ADR-0022 row is stated to land above Task 2's ADR-0023 row.
14. **Task 6 cross-reference.** "Task 12 step 6" → "Task 12 Step 9".
15. **Contract consolidated.** Every draft's "Contract additions" folded into the Contract section (layout extras, `pull_sheet` extras, `verify_local.py`, `board_names`/`board_state`/`board.py` extras, `STAMP_*`, `winPct`, the export and review helpers); per-task risk and design notes kept as **Notes** blocks; the superseded `--snapshot` default and layout shape listed.
16. **Checked, no change needed.** `harness.js` edits from Tasks 2, 3, 4 and 7 compose in order; `build_data.py` imports after Tasks 2 and 4; `Build.gs` edits from Tasks 2, 3 and 4; decision-log order (0021, 0022, 0023); every `AGENTS.md` old-string against the state the previous task leaves, with no rule stated twice; every `build-and-maintenance.md` old-string after Tasks 2, 8 and 9; engine behaviour Tasks 6 and 9 rely on; layout labels and keys Tasks 9 and 10 rely on; no committed file trips Task 1's `SECRET_RE` or `SHEET_RE` (`check-no-data.sh --tracked` exit 0 after every task).

### Review fixes applied after assembly

17. **Task 12 Step 3b (new): record before the Full rebuild.** `buildDraftBoard` recreates Board and Settings and carries only GONE, MINE and Notes across. `hand_record.py` pulls the Board player, GP, My GP Est, GP Y-1/2/3, XRank and Notes columns and every Settings input, one column per range (JSON with the auth header, or CSV), saves the record under `data/draft-board/pulls/`, prints counts and differing Settings keys, and exits 3 to stop for the owner, naming players in chat only. Step 5 now diffs GONE as well as MINE by name and compares the record before and after.
18. **Task 8 `_compare_draft`: tie groups as sets in displayed order.** A row is out of order only when its sorted value differs from the engine's at that position. New tests: two tied rows swapped pass; two untied rows swapped fail with row numbers. `fake_pulls` takes `row_order`.
19. **Task 8 tests: a varied board.** `varied()` gives the sources different orders, a tie in HBP ZSH (applied), and ticks below the top, using `set_values` and leaving `make_snapshot`'s defaults alone. It passes, and a broken disagreement condition fails with exit 1.
20. **Task 12 Step 8.2b (new):** clear the copy's inherited MINE, GONE and Punted ticks, then pull and verify at zero (tick counts, punted count, exit 0). Task 8's procedure gains the same step 0.
21. **Task 12 Step 8 failure loop, written out:** keep pulls and triage; delete the failed copy; engine-only fixes re-verify the cached scenario pulls first; `Build.gs` fixes redeploy to the live sheet and pass Steps 3b, 5 and 6 again; fresh copy; owner consent again; resume from 8.2b.
22. **Task 12 Step 8.4: deleting the copy** by exact title (`COPY_TITLE` read from the tab), Move to trash, then Delete forever on that single item in Trash; never Empty trash; confirmed by searching the title again, checking Trash and opening the copy's URL.
23. **Task 12 Step 8.3e: clean rows.** `swap_rows.py` picks two swap rows and one override row with no ticks, notes or hand values, and prints their row numbers; the step explains that I1 would otherwise move ticks while verify still passed. Task 8's procedure says the same.
24. **Task 8 `pull_sheet.assemble`: truncated sparse replies.** A short reply ending on an empty row is refused, and the Notes explain why a lost value still cannot become silent engine input (Draft Board comparison; hand columns mirrored on the Draft Board). New tests for the refusal and for a lost Board My GP caught through its mirror.
25. **Task 12 Step 11: C1 closed in writing.** Exact replacements for the bug record's status line and its "In the sheet: not yet" paragraph, committed with the branch summary.
26. **Task 12 Step 9:** `tracker` runs after `undo`, so the "0 players ticked" check has a command.
27. **Task 12 Step 6b (new): tie order on the live pull.** `tie_order.py` prints counts and a verdict for the `#` column and Punts. The branch summary gains the result and a follow-up item kept only if the verdict is EQUAL; no code change on this branch.
28. **Task 12 Notes and branch summary:** `playwright-cli` snapshots and screenshots land in the gitignored `.playwright-cli/`, which holds sheet text and the URL; screenshots are kept only under `data/draft-board/` or `/tmp`.
29. **ADR-0022 (Task 4 Step 10):** one sentence recording the bootstrap exception, where `Build.gs` changes before `board_engine.py` exists, and the same-commit rule from the engine's commit on.
30. **Task 12 Step 3 fallback:** three single-column CSV reads (names, MINE, GONE) through `ticks_from_csv.py`. The CSV route without the auth header is kept, because it works on this sheet.
31. **Not applied, by decision:** ADR-0023's rationale is unchanged (see the Open item row); Task 9, `rebase` and the `xrank`/`gp1`–`gp3` commands stay.
